74 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {
75 |       // Reverse the guards, so that they have the same order in the result.
76 |       addGuards(((ComposeWarningsGuard) guard).guards.descendingSet());
77 |     } else {
78 |       numberOfAdds++;
79 |       orderOfAddition.put(guard, numberOfAdds);
80 |       guards.remove(guard);
81 |       guards.add(guard);
82 |     }
83 |   }

===== M0006 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 286-310
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
286 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
287 |     if (prototype == null) {
288 |       return false;
289 |     }
290 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
291 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
292 |       return false;
293 |     }
294 | 
295 |     boolean replacedPrototype = prototype != null;
296 |     this.prototype = prototype;
297 | 
298 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
299 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
300 |       if (superClass != null) {
301 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
302 |       }
303 |     }
304 | 
305 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
306 |       clearCachedValues();
307 |     }
308 | 
309 |     return true;
310 |   }

===== M0014 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 723-866
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
723 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
724 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
725 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
726 |     // that we know to be safe
727 |     switch (n.getType()) {
728 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
729 |       case Token.AND:
730 |       case Token.BLOCK:
731 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
732 |       case Token.HOOK:
733 |       case Token.IF:
734 |       case Token.IN:
735 |       case Token.LP:
736 |       case Token.NUMBER:
737 |       case Token.OR:
738 |       case Token.THIS:
739 |       case Token.TRUE:
740 |       case Token.FALSE:
741 |       case Token.NULL:
742 |       case Token.STRING:
743 |       case Token.SWITCH:
744 |       case Token.TRY:
745 |       case Token.EMPTY:
746 |         break;
747 | 
748 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
749 |       case Token.THROW:
750 |         return true;
751 | 
752 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
753 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
754 |           return true;
755 |         }
756 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
757 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
758 |                   c.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
759 |             return true;
760 |           }
761 |         }
762 |         return false;
763 | 
764 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
765 |       case Token.REGEXP:
766 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
767 |           return true;
768 |         }
769 |         break;
770 | 
771 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
772 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
773 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
774 |           return true;
775 |         }
776 |         break;
777 | 
778 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
779 |         // Function expressions don't have side-effects, but function
780 |         // declarations change the namespace. Either way, we don't need to
781 |         // check the children, since they aren't executed at declaration time.
782 |         return checkForNewObjects || !isFunctionExpression(n);
783 | 
784 |       case Token.NEW:
785 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
786 |           return true;
787 |         }
788 | 
789 |         if (!constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
790 |           // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
791 |           // side-effects
792 |           break;
793 |         }
794 |         return true;
795 | 
796 |       case Token.CALL:
797 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
798 |         // side effect property set.
799 |         if (!functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler)) {
800 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
801 |           // side-effects
802 |           break;
803 |         }
804 |         return true;
805 | 
806 |       default:
807 |         if (isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType())) {
808 |           break;
809 |         }
810 | 
811 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
812 |           Node assignTarget = n.getFirstChild();
813 |           if (isName(assignTarget)) {
814 |             return true;
815 |           }
816 | 
817 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
818 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
819 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
820 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
821 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
822 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler) ||
823 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
824 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
825 |             return true;
826 |           }
827 | 
828 |           if (isGet(assignTarget)) {
829 |             // If the object being assigned to is a local object, don't
830 |             // consider this a side-effect as it can't be referenced
831 |             // elsewhere.  Don't do this recursively as the property might
832 |             // be an alias of another object, unlike a literal below.
833 |             Node current = assignTarget.getFirstChild();
834 |             if (evaluatesToLocalValue(current)) {
835 |               return false;
836 |             }
837 | 
838 |             // A literal value as defined by "isLiteralValue" is guaranteed
839 |             // not to be an alias, or any components which are aliases of
840 |             // other objects.
841 |             // If the root object is a literal don't consider this a
842 |             // side-effect.
843 |             while (isGet(current)) {
844 |               current = current.getFirstChild();
845 |             }
846 | 
847 |             return !isLiteralValue(current, true);
848 |           } else {
849 |             // TODO(johnlenz): remove this code and make this an exception. This
850 |             // is here only for legacy reasons, the AST is not valid but
851 |             // preserve existing behavior.
852 |             return !isLiteralValue(assignTarget, true);
853 |           }
854 |         }
855 | 
856 |         return true;
857 |     }
858 | 
859 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
860 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
861 |         return true;
862 |       }
863 |     }
864 | 
865 |     return false;
866 |   }

===== M0036 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1158-1169
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1158 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1159 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1160 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1161 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1162 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1163 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1164 |       } else {
1165 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1166 |       }
1167 |     }
1168 |     return builder.build();
1169 |   }

===== M0042 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 467-481
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
467 |   @Override
468 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
469 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
470 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
471 |     if (unknown) {
472 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
473 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
474 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
475 |         unknown = false;
476 |       } else {
477 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
478 |       }
479 |     }
480 |     return unknown;
481 |   }

===== M0045 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.strEscape(String,char,String,String,String,CharsetEncoder)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 988-1058
Documentation:
/** Helper to escape javascript string as well as regular expression */
Method code:
988 |   static String strEscape(String s, char quote,
989 |                           String doublequoteEscape,
990 |                           String singlequoteEscape,
991 |                           String backslashEscape,
992 |                           CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder) {
993 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(s.length() + 2);
994 |     sb.append(quote);
995 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
996 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
997 |       switch (c) {
998 |         case '\0': sb.append("\\0"); break;
999 |         case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
1000 |         case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
1001 |         case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
1002 |         case '\\': sb.append(backslashEscape); break;
1003 |         case '\"': sb.append(doublequoteEscape); break;
1004 |         case '\'': sb.append(singlequoteEscape); break;
1005 |         case '>':                       // Break --> into --\> or ]]> into ]]\>
1006 |           if (i >= 2 &&
1007 |               ((s.charAt(i - 1) == '-' && s.charAt(i - 2) == '-') ||
1008 |                (s.charAt(i - 1) == ']' && s.charAt(i - 2) == ']'))) {
1009 |             sb.append("\\>");
1010 |           } else {
1011 |             sb.append(c);
1012 |           }
1013 |           break;
1014 |         case '<':
1015 |           // Break </script into <\/script
1016 |           final String END_SCRIPT = "/script";
1017 | 
1018 |           // Break <!-- into <\!--
1019 |           final String START_COMMENT = "!--";
1020 | 
1021 |           if (s.regionMatches(true, i + 1, END_SCRIPT, 0,
1022 |                               END_SCRIPT.length())) {
1023 |             sb.append("<\\");
1024 |           } else if (s.regionMatches(false, i + 1, START_COMMENT, 0,
1025 |                                      START_COMMENT.length())) {
1026 |             sb.append("<\\");
1027 |           } else {
1028 |             sb.append(c);
1029 |           }
1030 |           break;
1031 |         default:
1032 |           // If we're given an outputCharsetEncoder, then check if the
1033 |           //  character can be represented in this character set.
1034 |           if (outputCharsetEncoder != null) {
1035 |             if (outputCharsetEncoder.canEncode(c)) {
1036 |               sb.append(c);
1037 |             } else {
1038 |               // Unicode-escape the character.
1039 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1040 |             }
1041 |           } else {
1042 |             // No charsetEncoder provided - pass straight latin characters
1043 |             // through, and escape the rest.  Doing the explicit character
1044 |             // check is measurably faster than using the CharsetEncoder.
1045 |             if (c > 0x1f && c <= 0x7f) {
1046 |               sb.append(c);
1047 |             } else {
1048 |               // Other characters can be misinterpreted by some js parsers,
1049 |               // or perhaps mangled by proxies along the way,
1050 |               // so we play it safe and unicode escape them.
1051 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1052 |             }
1053 |           }
1054 |       }
1055 |     }
1056 |     sb.append(quote);
1057 |     return sb.toString();
1058 |   }

===== M0054 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1160-1168
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1160 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1161 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1162 |       return -1;
1163 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1164 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1165 |     } else {
1166 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1167 |     }
1168 |   }

1454 |         options.getLanguageOut() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT);

1456 |

===== M0063 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldAssign(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 450-520
Documentation:

Method code:
450 |   private Node tryFoldAssign(Node n, Node left, Node right) {
451 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.getType() == Token.ASSIGN);
452 | 
453 |     // Tries to convert x = x + y -> x += y;
454 |     if (!right.hasChildren() ||
455 |         right.getFirstChild().getNext() != right.getLastChild()) {
456 |       // RHS must have two children.
457 |       return n;
458 |     }
459 | 
460 |     if (mayHaveSideEffects(left)) {
461 |       return n;
462 |     }
463 | 
464 |     Node newRight;
465 |     if (areNodesEqualForInlining(left, right.getFirstChild())) {
466 |       newRight = right.getLastChild();
467 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isCommutative(right.getType()) &&
468 |           areNodesEqualForInlining(left, right.getLastChild())) {
469 |       newRight = right.getFirstChild();
470 |     } else {
471 |       return n;
472 |     }
473 | 
474 |     int newType = -1;
475 |     switch (right.getType()) {
476 |       case Token.ADD:
477 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_ADD;
478 |         break;
479 |       case Token.BITAND:
480 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_BITAND;
481 |         break;
482 |       case Token.BITOR:
483 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_BITOR;
484 |         break;
485 |       case Token.BITXOR:
486 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR;
487 |         break;
488 |       case Token.DIV:
489 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_DIV;
490 |         break;
491 |       case Token.LSH:
492 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_LSH;
493 |         break;
494 |       case Token.MOD:
495 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_MOD;
496 |         break;
497 |       case Token.MUL:
498 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_MUL;
499 |         break;
500 |       case Token.RSH:
501 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_RSH;
502 |         break;
503 |       case Token.SUB:
504 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_SUB;
505 |         break;
506 |       case Token.URSH:
507 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_URSH;
508 |         break;
509 |       default:
510 |         return n;
511 |     }
512 | 
513 |     Node newNode = new Node(newType,
514 |         left.detachFromParent(), newRight.detachFromParent());
515 |     n.getParent().replaceChild(n, newNode);
516 | 
517 |     reportCodeChange();
518 | 
519 |     return newNode;
520 |   }

Method code:
176 |   private void tryReduceOperandsForOp(Node n) {
177 |     switch (n.getType()) {
178 |       case Token.ADD:
179 |         Node left = n.getFirstChild();
180 |         Node right = n.getLastChild();
181 |         if (!NodeUtil.mayBeString(left) && !NodeUtil.mayBeString(right)) {
182 |           tryConvertOperandsToNumber(n);
183 |         }
184 |         break;
185 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
186 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
187 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
188 |         // TODO(johnlenz): convert these to integers.
189 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
190 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
191 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
192 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
193 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
194 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
195 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
196 |         tryConvertToNumber(n.getLastChild());
197 |         break;
198 |       case Token.BITNOT:
199 |       case Token.BITOR:
200 |       case Token.BITXOR:
201 |       case Token.BITAND:
202 |       case Token.LSH:
203 |       case Token.RSH:
204 |       case Token.URSH:
205 |       case Token.SUB:
206 |       case Token.MUL:
207 |       case Token.MOD:
208 |       case Token.DIV:
209 |       case Token.POS:
210 |       case Token.NEG:
211 |         tryConvertOperandsToNumber(n);
212 |         break;
213 |     }
214 |   }

===== M0100 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 95-156
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
95 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
96 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
97 |     // just bail out early here.
98 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
99 |       return this;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
103 | 
104 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
105 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
106 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
107 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
108 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
109 |     }
110 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
111 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
112 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
113 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
114 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
115 |         }
116 |       } else {
117 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
118 |           return this;
119 |         }
120 | 
121 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
122 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
123 |         // one another.
124 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
125 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
126 |           JSType current = it.next();
127 | 
128 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
129 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
130 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
131 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
132 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
133 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
134 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
135 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
136 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
137 |               return this;
138 |             }
139 |           } else {
140 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
141 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
142 |               return this;
143 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
144 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
145 |               it.remove();
146 |             }
147 |           }
148 |         }
149 |         alternates.add(alternate);
150 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
151 |       }
152 |     } else {
153 |       result = null;
154 |     }
155 |     return this;
156 |   }

===== M0105 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1100-1174
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1100 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1101 |     switch (type) {
1102 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1103 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1104 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1105 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1106 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1107 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1108 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1109 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1110 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1111 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1112 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1113 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1114 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1115 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1116 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1117 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1118 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1119 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1120 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1121 |       case Token.EQ:
1122 |       case Token.NE:
1123 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1124 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1125 |       case Token.LT:
1126 |       case Token.GT:
1127 |       case Token.LE:
1128 |       case Token.GE:
1129 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1130 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1131 |       case Token.LSH:
1132 |       case Token.RSH:
1133 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1134 |       case Token.SUB:
1135 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1136 |       case Token.MUL:
1137 |       case Token.MOD:
1138 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1139 |       case Token.INC:
1140 |       case Token.DEC:
1141 |       case Token.NEW:
1142 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1143 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1144 |       case Token.VOID:
1145 |       case Token.NOT:
1146 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1147 |       case Token.POS:
1148 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1149 | 
1150 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1151 |       case Token.CALL:
1152 |       case Token.EMPTY:
1153 |       case Token.FALSE:
1154 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1155 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1156 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1157 |       case Token.GET_REF:
1158 |       case Token.IF:
1159 |       case Token.LP:
1160 |       case Token.NAME:
1161 |       case Token.NULL:
1162 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1163 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1164 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1165 |       case Token.STRING:
1166 |       case Token.THIS:
1167 |       case Token.TRUE:
1168 |         return 15;
1169 | 
1170 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1171 |                                Node.tokenToName(type) +
1172 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1173 |     }
1174 |   }

===== M0126 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldUnaryOperator(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 325-416
Documentation:

Method code:
325 |   private Node tryFoldUnaryOperator(Node n) {
326 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.hasOneChild());
327 | 
328 |     Node left = n.getFirstChild();
329 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
330 | 
331 |     if (left == null) {
332 |       return n;
333 |     }
334 | 
335 |     TernaryValue leftVal = NodeUtil.getPureBooleanValue(left);
336 |     if (leftVal == TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
337 |       return n;
338 |     }
339 | 
340 |     switch (n.getType()) {
341 |       case Token.NOT:
342 |         // Don't fold !0 and !1 back to false.
343 |         if (left.getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
344 |           double numValue = left.getDouble();
345 |           if (numValue == 0 || numValue == 1) {
346 |             return n;
347 |           }
348 |         }
349 |         int result = leftVal.toBoolean(true) ? Token.FALSE : Token.TRUE;
350 |         Node replacementNode = new Node(result);
351 |         parent.replaceChild(n, replacementNode);
352 |         reportCodeChange();
353 |         return replacementNode;
354 |       case Token.POS:
355 |         if (NodeUtil.isNumericResult(left)) {
356 |           // POS does nothing to numeric values.
357 |           parent.replaceChild(n, left.detachFromParent());
358 |           reportCodeChange();
359 |           return left;
360 |         }
361 |         return n;
362 |       case Token.NEG:
363 |         try {
364 |           if (left.getType() == Token.NAME) {
365 |             if (left.getString().equals("Infinity")) {
366 |               // "-Infinity" is valid and a literal, don't modify it.
367 |               return n;
368 |             } else if (left.getString().equals("NaN")) {
369 |               // "-NaN" is "NaN".
370 |               n.removeChild(left);
371 |               parent.replaceChild(n, left);
372 |               reportCodeChange();
373 |               return left;
374 |             }
375 |           }
376 | 
377 |           double negNum = -left.getDouble();
378 | 
379 |           Node negNumNode = Node.newNumber(negNum);
380 |           parent.replaceChild(n, negNumNode);
381 |           reportCodeChange();
382 |           return negNumNode;
383 |         } catch (UnsupportedOperationException ex) {
384 |           // left is not a number node, so do not replace, but warn the
385 |           // user because they can't be doing anything good
386 |           error(NEGATING_A_NON_NUMBER_ERROR, left);
387 |           return n;
388 |         }
389 |       case Token.BITNOT:
390 |         try {
391 |           double val = left.getDouble();
392 |           if (val >= Integer.MIN_VALUE && val <= Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
393 |             int intVal = (int) val;
394 |             if (intVal == val) {
395 |               Node notIntValNode = Node.newNumber(~intVal);
396 |               parent.replaceChild(n, notIntValNode);
397 |               reportCodeChange();
398 |               return notIntValNode;
399 |             } else {
400 |               error(FRACTIONAL_BITWISE_OPERAND, left);
401 |               return n;
402 |             }
403 |           } else {
404 |             error(BITWISE_OPERAND_OUT_OF_RANGE, left);
405 |             return n;
406 |           }
407 |         } catch (UnsupportedOperationException ex) {
408 |           // left is not a number node, so do not replace, but warn the
409 |           // user because they can't be doing anything good
410 |           error(NEGATING_A_NON_NUMBER_ERROR, left);
411 |           return n;
412 |         }
413 |         default:
414 |           return n;
415 |     }
416 |   }

/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
417 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
418 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
419 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
420 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
421 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
422 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
423 |         String inputName = input.getName();
424 | 
425 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
426 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
427 |         // will get an error down the line.
428 |         inputs.add(input);
429 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
430 |       }
431 |     }
432 |     return inputs;
433 |   }

327 |   private int position2charno(int position) {
328 |     int lineIndex = sourceString.lastIndexOf('\n', position);
329 |     if (lineIndex == -1) {
330 |       return position;
331 |     } else {
332 |       // Subtract one for initial position being 0.

334 |     }

===== M0139 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldComparison(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 887-1069
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to fold comparison nodes, e.g ==
   */
Method code:
887 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
888 |   private Node tryFoldComparison(Node n, Node left, Node right) {
889 |     if (!NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(left, false) ||
890 |         !NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(right, false)) {
891 |       // We only handle non-literal operands for LT and GT.
892 |       if (n.getType() != Token.GT && n.getType() != Token.LT) {
893 |         return n;
894 |       }
895 |     }
896 | 
897 |     int op = n.getType();
898 |     boolean result;
899 | 
900 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Use the JSType to compare nodes of different types.
901 | 
902 |     boolean rightLiteral = NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(right, false);
903 |     boolean undefinedRight = ((Token.NAME == right.getType()
904 |           && right.getString().equals("undefined"))
905 |           || (Token.VOID == right.getType()
906 |               && NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(right.getFirstChild(), false)));
907 |     int lhType = left.getType();
908 |     int rhType = right.getType();
909 |     switch (lhType) {
910 |       case Token.VOID:
911 |         if (!NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(left.getFirstChild(), false)) {
912 |           return n;
913 |         } else if (!rightLiteral) {
914 |           return n;
915 |         } else {
916 |           result = compareToUndefined(right, op);
917 |         }
918 |         break;
919 | 
920 |       case Token.NULL:
921 |       case Token.TRUE:
922 |       case Token.FALSE:
923 |         if (undefinedRight) {
924 |           result = compareToUndefined(left, op);
925 |           break;
926 |         }
927 |         if (rhType != Token.TRUE &&
928 |             rhType != Token.FALSE &&
929 |             rhType != Token.NULL) {
930 |           return n;
931 |         }
932 |         switch (op) {
933 |           case Token.SHEQ:
934 |           case Token.EQ:
935 |             result = lhType == rhType;
936 |             break;
937 | 
938 |           case Token.SHNE:
939 |           case Token.NE:
940 |             result = lhType != rhType;
941 |             break;
942 | 
943 |           case Token.GE:
944 |           case Token.LE:
945 |           case Token.GT:
946 |           case Token.LT:
947 |             Boolean compareResult = compareAsNumbers(op, left, right);
948 |             if (compareResult != null) {
949 |               result = compareResult;
950 |             } else {
951 |               return n;
952 |             }
953 |             break;
954 | 
955 |           default:
956 |             return n;  // we only handle == and != here
957 |         }
958 |         break;
959 | 
960 |       case Token.THIS:
961 |         if (right.getType() != Token.THIS) {
962 |           return n;
963 |         }
964 |         switch (op) {
965 |           case Token.SHEQ:
966 |           case Token.EQ:
967 |             result = true;
968 |             break;
969 | 
970 |           case Token.SHNE:
971 |           case Token.NE:
972 |             result = false;
973 |             break;
974 | 
975 |           // We can only handle == and != here.
976 |           // GT, LT, GE, LE depend on the type of "this" and how it will
977 |           // be converted to number.  The results are different depending on
978 |           // whether it is a string, NaN or other number value.
979 |           default:
980 |             return n;
981 |         }
982 |         break;
983 | 
984 |       case Token.STRING:
985 |         if (undefinedRight) {
986 |           result = compareToUndefined(left, op);
987 |           break;
988 |         }
989 |         if (Token.STRING != right.getType()) {
990 |           return n;  // Only eval if they are the same type
991 |         }
992 |         switch (op) {
993 |           case Token.SHEQ:
994 |           case Token.EQ:
995 |             result = left.getString().equals(right.getString());
996 |             break;
997 | 
998 |           case Token.SHNE:
999 |           case Token.NE:
1000 |             result = !left.getString().equals(right.getString());
1001 |             break;
1002 | 
1003 |           default:
1004 |             return n;  // we only handle == and != here
1005 |         }
1006 |         break;
1007 | 
1008 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1009 |         if (undefinedRight) {
1010 |           result = compareToUndefined(left, op);
1011 |           break;
1012 |         }
1013 |         if (Token.NUMBER != right.getType()) {
1014 |           return n;  // Only eval if they are the same type
1015 |         }
1016 |         Boolean compareResult = compareAsNumbers(op, left, right);
1017 |         if (compareResult != null) {
1018 |           result = compareResult;
1019 |         } else {
1020 |           return null;
1021 |         }
1022 |         break;
1023 | 
1024 |       case Token.NAME:
1025 |         if (undefinedRight) {
1026 |           result = compareToUndefined(left, op);
1027 |           break;
1028 |         }
1029 | 
1030 |         if (rightLiteral) {
1031 |           boolean undefinedLeft = (left.getString().equals("undefined"));
1032 |           if (undefinedLeft) {
1033 |             result = compareToUndefined(right, op);
1034 |             break;
1035 |           }
1036 |         }
1037 | 
1038 |         if (Token.NAME != right.getType()) {
1039 |           return n;  // Only eval if they are the same type
1040 |         }
1041 |         String ln = left.getString();
1042 |         String rn = right.getString();
1043 |         if (!ln.equals(rn)) {
1044 |           return n;  // Not the same value name.
1045 |         }
1046 | 
1047 |         switch (op) {
1048 |           // If we knew the named value wouldn't be NaN, it would be nice
1049 |           // to handle EQ,NE,LE,GE,SHEQ, and SHNE.
1050 |           case Token.LT:
1051 |           case Token.GT:
1052 |             result = false;
1053 |             break;
1054 |           default:
1055 |             return n;  // don't handle that op
1056 |         }
1057 |         break;
1058 | 
1059 |       default:
1060 |         // assert, this should cover all consts
1061 |         return n;
1062 |     }
1063 | 
1064 |     Node newNode = new Node(result ? Token.TRUE : Token.FALSE);
1065 |     n.getParent().replaceChild(n, newNode);
1066 |     reportCodeChange();
1067 | 
1068 |     return newNode;
1069 |   }

/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
406 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
407 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
408 | 
409 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
410 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
411 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
412 |       return 0;
413 |     }
414 | 
415 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
416 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
417 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
418 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
419 |       // have been added
420 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
421 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
422 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
423 |       }
424 |     }
425 | 
426 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
427 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
428 |       return 0;
429 |     }
430 | 
431 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
432 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
433 |   }

===== M0155 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getPureBooleanValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 119-159
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the boolean value of a node that represents a literal. This method
   * effectively emulates the <code>Boolean()</code> JavaScript cast function
   * except it return UNKNOWN for known values with side-effects, use
   * getExpressionBooleanValue if you don't care about side-effects.
   */
Method code:
119 |   static TernaryValue getPureBooleanValue(Node n) {
120 |     switch (n.getType()) {
121 |       case Token.STRING:
122 |         return TernaryValue.forBoolean(n.getString().length() > 0);
123 | 
124 |       case Token.NUMBER:
125 |         return TernaryValue.forBoolean(n.getDouble() != 0);
126 | 
127 |       case Token.NOT:
128 |         return getPureBooleanValue(n.getLastChild()).not();
129 | 
130 |       case Token.NULL:
131 |       case Token.FALSE:
132 |       case Token.VOID:
133 |         return TernaryValue.FALSE;
134 | 
135 |       case Token.NAME:
136 |         String name = n.getString();
137 |         if ("undefined".equals(name)
138 |             || "NaN".equals(name)) {
139 |           // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
140 |           // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
141 |           return TernaryValue.FALSE;
142 |         } else if ("Infinity".equals(name)) {
143 |           return TernaryValue.TRUE;
144 |         }
145 |         break;
146 | 
147 |       case Token.TRUE:
148 |       case Token.REGEXP:
149 |         return TernaryValue.TRUE;
150 | 
151 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
152 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
153 |         if (!mayHaveSideEffects(n)) {
154 |           return TernaryValue.TRUE;
155 |         }
156 |     }
157 | 
158 |     return TernaryValue.UNKNOWN;
159 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)

Original range: 607-619
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
607 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
608 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
609 |     if (typeSet == null) {
610 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
611 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
612 |     }
613 | 
614 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
615 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
616 | 
617 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
618 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
619 |   }

===== M0169 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldBinaryOperator(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 96-163
Documentation:

Method code:
96 |   private Node tryFoldBinaryOperator(Node subtree) {
97 |     Node left = subtree.getFirstChild();
98 | 
99 |     if (left == null) {
100 |       return subtree;
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     Node right = left.getNext();
104 | 
105 |     if (right == null) {
106 |       return subtree;
107 |     }
108 | 
109 |     // If we've reached here, node is truly a binary operator.
110 |     switch(subtree.getType()) {
111 |       case Token.GETPROP:
112 |         return tryFoldGetProp(subtree, left, right);
113 | 
114 |       case Token.GETELEM:
115 |         return tryFoldGetElem(subtree, left, right);
116 | 
117 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
118 |         return tryFoldInstanceof(subtree, left, right);
119 | 
120 |       case Token.AND:
121 |       case Token.OR:
122 |         return tryFoldAndOr(subtree, left, right);
123 | 
124 |       case Token.LSH:
125 |       case Token.RSH:
126 |       case Token.URSH:
127 |         return tryFoldShift(subtree, left, right);
128 | 
129 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
130 |         return tryFoldAssign(subtree, left, right);
131 | 
132 |       case Token.ADD:
133 |         return tryFoldAdd(subtree, left, right);
134 | 
135 |       case Token.SUB:
136 |       case Token.DIV:
137 |       case Token.MOD:
138 |         return tryFoldArithmeticOp(subtree, left, right);
139 | 
140 |       case Token.MUL:
141 |       case Token.BITAND:
142 |       case Token.BITOR:
143 |       case Token.BITXOR:
144 |         Node result = tryFoldArithmeticOp(subtree, left, right);
145 |         if (result != subtree) {
146 |           return result;
147 |         }
148 |         return tryFoldLeftChildOp(subtree, left, right);
149 | 
150 |       case Token.LT:
151 |       case Token.GT:
152 |       case Token.LE:
153 |       case Token.GE:
154 |       case Token.EQ:
155 |       case Token.NE:
156 |       case Token.SHEQ:
157 |       case Token.SHNE:
158 |         return tryFoldComparison(subtree, left, right);
159 | 
160 |       default:
161 |         return subtree;
162 |     }
163 |   }

===== M0173 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 474-497
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
474 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
475 |     switch (n.getType()) {
476 |       case Token.STRING:
477 |       case Token.NUMBER:
478 |       case Token.NULL:
479 |       case Token.TRUE:
480 |       case Token.FALSE:
481 |         return true;
482 |       case Token.NOT:
483 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
484 |       case Token.VOID:
485 |       case Token.NEG:
486 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
487 |       case Token.NAME:
488 |         String name = n.getString();
489 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
490 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
491 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
492 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
493 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
494 |     }
495 | 
496 |     return false;
497 |   }

89 |     @Override
90 |     void startSourceMapping(Node node) {

===== M0179 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.optimizeSubtree(Node)

Method code:
68 |   @Override
69 |   Node optimizeSubtree(Node subtree) {
70 |     switch(subtree.getType()) {
71 |       case Token.CALL:
72 |         return tryFoldKnownMethods(subtree);
73 | 
74 |       case Token.NEW:
75 |         return tryFoldCtorCall(subtree);
76 | 
77 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
78 |         return tryFoldTypeof(subtree);
79 | 
80 |       case Token.NOT:
81 |       case Token.POS:
82 |       case Token.NEG:
83 |       case Token.BITNOT:
84 |         tryReduceOperandsForOp(subtree);
85 |         return tryFoldUnaryOperator(subtree);
86 | 
87 |       case Token.VOID:
88 |         return tryReduceVoid(subtree);
89 | 
90 |       default:
91 |         tryReduceOperandsForOp(subtree);
92 |         return tryFoldBinaryOperator(subtree);
93 |     }
94 |   }

===== M0181 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 888-915
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
888 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
889 |     // unknown
890 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
891 |       return true;
892 |     }
893 |     // equality
894 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
895 |       return true;
896 |     }
897 |     // all type
898 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
899 |       return true;
900 |     }
901 |     // unions
902 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
903 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
904 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
905 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
906 |           return true;
907 |         }
908 |       }
909 |     }
910 |     // named types
911 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
912 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
913 |     }
914 |     return false;
915 |   }

===== M0182 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeCutLine()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 459-475
Documentation:
/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
459 |     @Override
460 |     void maybeCutLine() {
461 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
462 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
463 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
464 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
465 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
466 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
467 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition);
468 |           lineIndex++;
469 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
470 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
471 |         } else {
472 |           startNewLine();
473 |         }
474 |       }
475 |     }

/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

===== M0188 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryConvertToNumber(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 224-266
Documentation:

Method code:
224 |   private void tryConvertToNumber(Node n) {
225 |     switch (n.getType()) {
226 |       case Token.NUMBER:
227 |         // Nothing to do
228 |         return;
229 |       case Token.AND:
230 |       case Token.OR:
231 |       case Token.COMMA:
232 |         tryConvertToNumber(n.getLastChild());
233 |         return;
234 |       case Token.HOOK:
235 |         tryConvertToNumber(n.getChildAtIndex(1));
236 |         tryConvertToNumber(n.getLastChild());
237 |         return;
238 |       case Token.NAME:
239 |         if (!NodeUtil.isUndefined(n)) {
240 |           return;
241 |         }
242 |         break;
243 |     }
244 | 
245 |     Double result = NodeUtil.getNumberValue(n);
246 |     if (result == null) {
247 |       return;
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     double value = result;
251 | 
252 |     Node replacement;
253 |     if (Double.isNaN(value)) {
254 |       replacement = Node.newString(Token.NAME, "NaN");
255 |     } else if (value == Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY) {
256 |       replacement = Node.newString(Token.NAME, "Infinity");
257 |     } else if (value == Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY) {
258 |       replacement = new Node(Token.NEG, Node.newString(Token.NAME, "Infinity"));
259 |       replacement.copyInformationFromForTree(n);
260 |     } else {
261 |       replacement = Node.newNumber(value);
262 |     }
263 | 
264 |     n.getParent().replaceChild(n, replacement);
265 |     reportCodeChange();
266 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processUnaryExpression(UnaryExpression)

Method code:
894 |     @Override
895 |     Node processUnaryExpression(UnaryExpression exprNode) {
896 |       int type = transformTokenType(exprNode.getType());
897 |       Node operand = transform(exprNode.getOperand());
898 |       if (type == Token.NEG && operand.getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
899 |         operand.setDouble(-operand.getDouble());
900 |         return operand;
901 |       } else {
902 |         if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
903 |           if (!validAssignmentTarget(operand)) {
904 |             String msg = (type == Token.INC)
905 |                 ? "invalid increment target"
906 |                 : "invalid decrement target";
907 |             errorReporter.error(
908 |               msg,
909 |               sourceName,
910 |               operand.getLineno(), "", 0);
911 |           }
912 |         }
913 | 
914 |         Node node = newNode(type, operand);
915 |         if (exprNode.isPostfix()) {
916 |           node.putBooleanProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP, true);
917 |         }
918 |         return node;
919 |       }
920 |     }

===== M0198 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 233-259
Documentation:

Method code:
233 |   void addNumber(double x) {
234 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
235 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
236 |     char prev = getLastChar();
237 |     if (x < 0 && prev == '-') {
238 |       add(" ");
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     if ((long) x == x) {
242 |       long value = (long) x;
243 |       long mantissa = value;
244 |       int exp = 0;
245 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
246 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
247 |           mantissa /= 10;
248 |           exp++;
249 |         }
250 |       }
251 |       if (exp > 2) {
252 |         add(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
253 |       } else {
254 |         add(Long.toString(value));
255 |       }
256 |     } else {
257 |       add(String.valueOf(x));
258 |     }
259 |   }

===== M0202 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 221-279
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
221 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
222 |     this.options = options;
223 |     if (errorManager == null) {
224 |       if (outStream == null) {
225 |         setErrorManager(
226 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
227 |       } else {
228 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
229 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
230 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
231 |         setErrorManager(printer);
232 |       }
233 |     }
234 | 
235 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
236 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
237 |       options.checkTypes = true;
238 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
239 |       options.checkTypes = false;
240 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
241 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
242 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
243 |       // parser type warnings.
244 |       options.setWarningLevel(
245 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
246 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
247 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn()) {
251 |       options.setWarningLevel(
252 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
253 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
257 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
258 |     guards.add(
259 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
260 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
261 |     WarningsGuard warningsGuard = options.getWarningsGuard();
262 |     if (warningsGuard != null) {
263 |       guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
267 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
268 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
269 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
270 |     // error off.
271 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
272 |         (warningsGuard == null || !warningsGuard.disables(
273 |             DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES))) {
274 |       guards.add(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
275 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
276 |     }
277 | 
278 |     this.warningsGuard = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
279 |   }

85 |       String sourceStr) {

88 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr,

2151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND: return "&=";

929 |       boolean printAnnotations,
930 |       boolean printType) {
931 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
932 |       sb.append(Token.name(type));
933 |       if (this instanceof StringNode) {
934 |         sb.append(' ');
935 |         sb.append(getString());
936 |       } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
937 |         sb.append(' ');
938 |         // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
939 |         // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
940 |         // toStringTree.
941 |         if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
942 |           sb.append("<invalid>");
943 |         } else {
944 |           sb.append(first.getString());
945 |         }
946 |       } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
947 |         ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode) this;
948 |         if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
949 |           FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode) this;
950 |           sb.append(' ');
951 |           sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
952 |         }
953 |         if (printSource) {
954 |           sb.append(" [source name: ");
955 |           sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
956 |           sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
957 |           sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd() - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
958 |           sb.append("] [base line: ");
959 |           sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
960 |           sb.append("] [end line: ");
961 |           sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
962 |           sb.append(']');
963 |         }
964 |       } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
965 |         sb.append(' ');
966 |         sb.append(getDouble());
967 |       }
968 |       if (printSource) {
969 |         int lineno = getLineno();
970 |         if (lineno != -1) {
971 |           sb.append(' ');
972 |           sb.append(lineno);
973 |         }
974 |       }
975 | 
976 |       if (printAnnotations) {
977 |         int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
978 |         for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
979 |           int type = keys[i];
980 |           PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
981 |           sb.append(" [");
982 |           sb.append(propToString(type));
983 |           sb.append(": ");
984 |           String value;
985 |           switch (type) {
986 |             case TARGETBLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it recurses
987 |               value = "target block property";
988 |               break;
989 |             case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it is dull
990 |               value = "last local block";
991 |               break;
992 |             case ISNUMBER_PROP:
993 |               switch (x.intValue) {
994 |                 case BOTH:
995 |                   value = "both";
996 |                   break;
997 |                 case RIGHT:
998 |                   value = "right";
999 |                   break;
1000 |                 case LEFT:
1001 |                   value = "left";
1002 |                   break;
1003 |                 default:
1004 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1005 |               }
1006 |               break;
1007 |             case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
1008 |               switch (x.intValue) {
1009 |                 case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
1010 |                   value = "eval";
1011 |                   break;
1012 |                 case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
1013 |                   value = "with";
1014 |                   break;
1015 |                 default:
1016 |                   // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
1017 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1018 |               }
1019 |               break;
1020 |             default:
1021 |               Object obj = x.objectValue;
1022 |               if (obj != null) {
1023 |                 value = obj.toString();
1024 |               } else {
1025 |                 value = String.valueOf(x.intValue);
1026 |               }
1027 |               break;
1028 |           }
1029 |           sb.append(value);
1030 |           sb.append(']');
1031 |         }
1032 |       }
1033 | 
1034 |       if (printType) {
1035 |         if (jsType != null) {
1036 |           String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1037 |           if (jsTypeString != null) {
1038 |             sb.append(" : ");
1039 |             sb.append(jsTypeString);
1040 |           }
1041 |         }
1042 |       }
1043 |     }
1044 |   }

===== M0220 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getStringNumberValue(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 316-360
Documentation:

Method code:
316 |   static Double getStringNumberValue(String rawJsString) {
317 |     if (rawJsString.contains("\u000b")) {
318 |       // vertical tab is not always whitespace
319 |       return null;
320 |     }
321 | 
322 |     String s = trimJsWhiteSpace(rawJsString);
323 |     // return ScriptRuntime.toNumber(s);
324 |     if (s.length() == 0) {
325 |       return 0.0;
326 |     }
327 | 
328 |     if (s.length() > 2
329 |         && s.charAt(0) == '0'
330 |         && (s.charAt(1) == 'x' || s.charAt(1) == 'X')) {
331 |       // Attempt to convert hex numbers.
332 |       try {
333 |         return Double.valueOf(Integer.parseInt(s.substring(2), 16));
334 |       } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
335 |         return Double.NaN;
336 |       }
337 |     }
338 | 
339 |     if (s.length() > 3
340 |         && (s.charAt(0) == '-' || s.charAt(0) == '+')
341 |         && s.charAt(1) == '0'
342 |         && (s.charAt(2) == 'x' || s.charAt(2) == 'X')) {
343 |       // hex numbers with explicit signs vary between browsers.
344 |       return null;
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     // FireFox and IE treat the "Infinity" differently. FireFox is case
348 |     // insensitive, but IE treats "infinity" as NaN.  So leave it alone.
349 |     if (s.equals("infinity")
350 |         || s.equals("-infinity")
351 |         || s.equals("+infinity")) {
352 |       return null;
353 |     }
354 | 
355 |     try {
356 |       return Double.parseDouble(s);
357 |     } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
358 |       return Double.NaN;
359 |     }
360 |   }

===== M0224 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2422-2432
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2422 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2423 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2424 |     int len = s.length();
2425 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2426 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2427 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2428 |         return false;
2429 |       }
2430 |     }
2431 |     return true;
2432 |   }

===== M0226 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.compareToUndefined(Node,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 1121-1146
Documentation:
/**
   * @param value The value to compare to "undefined"
   * @param op The boolean op to compare with
   * @return Whether the boolean op is true or false
   */
Method code:
1121 |   private boolean compareToUndefined(Node value, int op) {
1122 |     boolean valueUndefined = ((Token.NAME == value.getType()
1123 |         && value.getString().equals("undefined"))
1124 |         || (Token.VOID == value.getType()
1125 |             && NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(value.getFirstChild(), false)));
1126 |     boolean valueNull = (Token.NULL == value.getType());
1127 |     boolean equivalent = valueUndefined || valueNull;
1128 |     switch (op) {
1129 |       case Token.EQ:
1130 |         // undefined is only equal to null or an undefined value
1131 |         return equivalent;
1132 |       case Token.NE:
1133 |         return !equivalent;
1134 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1135 |         return valueUndefined;
1136 |       case Token.SHNE:
1137 |         return !valueUndefined;
1138 |       case Token.LT:
1139 |       case Token.GT:
1140 |       case Token.LE:
1141 |       case Token.GE:
1142 |         return false;
1143 |       default:
1144 |         throw new IllegalStateException("unexpected.");
1145 |     }
1146 |   }

===== M0246 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1060-1080
Documentation:

Method code:
1060 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1061 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1062 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1063 |       return s;
1064 |     }
1065 | 
1066 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1067 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1068 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1069 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1070 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1071 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1072 |       // crazy.
1073 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1074 |         sb.append(c);
1075 |       } else {
1076 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1077 |       }
1078 |     }
1079 |     return sb.toString();
1080 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
1889 |   public Node cloneTree() {
1890 |     Node result = cloneNode();
1891 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {
1892 |       Node n2clone = n2.cloneTree();
1893 |       n2clone.parent = result;
1894 |       if (result.last != null) {
1895 |         result.last.next = n2clone;
1896 |       }
1897 |       if (result.first == null) {
1898 |         result.first = n2clone;
1899 |       }
1900 |       result.last = n2clone;
1901 |     }
1902 |     return result;
1903 |   }

===== M0279 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 421-436
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
421 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
422 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
423 |       Set<String> directives = null;
424 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
425 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
426 |         if (directives == null) {
427 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
428 |         } else {
429 |           directives.add(directive);
430 |         }
431 |       }
432 | 
433 |       if (directives != null) {
434 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
435 |       }
436 |     }

===== M0287 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getNumberValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 257-314
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the value of a node as a Number, or null if it cannot be converted.
   * When it returns a non-null Double, this method effectively emulates the
   * <code>Number()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   */
Method code:
257 |   static Double getNumberValue(Node n) {
258 |     switch (n.getType()) {
259 |       case Token.TRUE:
260 |         return 1.0;
261 | 
262 |       case Token.FALSE:
263 |       case Token.NULL:
264 |         return 0.0;
265 | 
266 |       case Token.NUMBER:
267 |         return n.getDouble();
268 | 
269 |       case Token.VOID:
270 |         if (mayHaveSideEffects(n.getFirstChild())) {
271 |           return null;
272 |         } else {
273 |           return Double.NaN;
274 |         }
275 | 
276 |       case Token.NAME:
277 |         // Check for known constants
278 |         String name = n.getString();
279 |         if (name.equals("undefined")) {
280 |           return Double.NaN;
281 |         }
282 |         if (name.equals("NaN")) {
283 |           return Double.NaN;
284 |         }
285 |         if (name.equals("Infinity")) {
286 |           return Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
287 |         }
288 |         return null;
289 | 
290 |       case Token.NEG:
291 |         if (n.getChildCount() == 1 && n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME
292 |             && n.getFirstChild().getString().equals("Infinity")) {
293 |           return Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
294 |         }
295 |         return null;
296 | 
297 |       case Token.NOT:
298 |         TernaryValue child = getPureBooleanValue(n.getFirstChild());
299 |         if (child != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
300 |           return child.toBoolean(true) ? 0.0 : 1.0; // reversed.
301 |         }
302 |         break;
303 | 
304 |       case Token.STRING:
305 |         return getStringNumberValue(n.getString());
306 | 
307 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
308 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
309 |         String value = getStringValue(n);
310 |         return value != null ? getStringNumberValue(value) : null;
311 |     }
312 | 
313 |     return null;
314 |   }

180 |         }

193 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
194 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
195 |       case Token.DO:

Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1410 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1411 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1412 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1413 |     }
1414 | 
1415 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1416 |     Node n, n2;
1417 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1418 |          res == null && n != null;
1419 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1420 |       if (node2 == null) {
1421 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1422 |       }
1423 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1424 |       if (res != null) {
1425 |         return res;
1426 |       }
1427 |     }
1428 |     return res;
1429 |   }

===== M0307 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 422-466
Documentation:

Method code:
422 |   @Override
423 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
424 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
425 |       return true;
426 |     }
427 | 
428 |     // Union types
429 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
430 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
431 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
432 |       return false;
433 |     }
434 | 
435 |     // record types
436 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
437 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // Interfaces
441 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
442 |     // to the interface instance.
443 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
444 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
445 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
446 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
447 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
448 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
449 |           return true;
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 |     }
453 | 
454 |     // other prototype based objects
455 |     if (that != null) {
456 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
457 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
458 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
459 |         // to avoid guessing.
460 |         return true;
461 |       }
462 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
463 |     }
464 | 
465 |     return false;
466 |   }

===== M0315 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.compareAsNumbers(int,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 1079-1114
Documentation:
/**
   * The result of the comparison as a Boolean or null if the
   * result could not be determined.
   */
Method code:
1079 |   private Boolean compareAsNumbers(int op, Node left, Node right) {
1080 |     Double leftValue = NodeUtil.getNumberValue(left);
1081 |     if (leftValue == null) {
1082 |       return null;
1083 |     }
1084 |     Double rightValue = NodeUtil.getNumberValue(right);
1085 |     if (rightValue == null) {
1086 |       return null;
1087 |     }
1088 | 
1089 |     double lv = leftValue;
1090 |     double rv = rightValue;
1091 | 
1092 |     Boolean result;
1093 |     switch (op) {
1094 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1095 |       case Token.EQ:
1096 |         Preconditions.checkState(
1097 |             left.getType() == Token.NUMBER && right.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
1098 |         result = lv == rv;
1099 |         break;
1100 |       case Token.SHNE:
1101 |       case Token.NE:
1102 |         Preconditions.checkState(
1103 |             left.getType() == Token.NUMBER && right.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
1104 |         result = lv != rv;
1105 |         break;
1106 |       case Token.LE: result = lv <= rv; break;
1107 |       case Token.LT: result = lv <  rv; break;
1108 |       case Token.GE: result = lv >= rv; break;
1109 |       case Token.GT: result = lv >  rv; break;
1110 |       default:
1111 |         return null;  // don't handle that op
1112 |     }
1113 |     return result;
1114 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object

===== M0335 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 521-559
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is a literal value. We define a literal value
   * as any node that evaluates to the same thing regardless of when or
   * where it is evaluated. So /xyz/ and [3, 5] are literals, but
   * the name a is not.
   *
   * Function literals do not meet this definition, because they
   * lexically capture variables. For example, if you have
   * <code>
   * function() { return a; }
   * </code>
   * If it is evaluated in a different scope, then it
   * captures a different variable. Even if the function did not read
   * any captured vairables directly, it would still fail this definition,
   * because it affects the lifecycle of variables in the enclosing scope.
   *
   * However, a function literal with respect to a particular scope is
   * a literal.
   *
   * @param includeFunctions If true, all function expressions will be
   *     treated as literals.
   */
Method code:
521 |   static boolean isLiteralValue(Node n, boolean includeFunctions) {
522 |     switch (n.getType()) {
523 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
524 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
525 |              child = child.getNext()) {
526 |           if (child.getType() != Token.EMPTY
527 |               && !isLiteralValue(child, includeFunctions)) {
528 |             return false;
529 |           }
530 |         }
531 |         return true;
532 | 
533 |       case Token.REGEXP:
534 |         // Return true only if all children are const.
535 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
536 |              child = child.getNext()) {
537 |           if (!isLiteralValue(child, includeFunctions)) {
538 |             return false;
539 |           }
540 |         }
541 |         return true;
542 | 
543 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
544 |         // Return true only if all values are const.
545 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
546 |              child = child.getNext()) {
547 |           if (!isLiteralValue(child.getFirstChild(), includeFunctions)) {
548 |             return false;
549 |           }
550 |         }
551 |         return true;
552 | 
553 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
554 |         return includeFunctions && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n);
555 | 
556 |       default:
557 |         return isImmutableValue(n);
558 |     }
559 |   }

===== M0338 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 632-658
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
632 |   @Override
633 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
634 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
635 |       return false;
636 |     }
637 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
638 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
639 |       return false;
640 |     }
641 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
642 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
643 |         return this == that;
644 |       }
645 |       return false;
646 |     }
647 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
648 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
649 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
650 |       }
651 |       return false;
652 |     }
653 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
654 |       return false;
655 |     }
656 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
657 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
658 |   }

===== M0339 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 163-184
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
163 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
164 |     if (isAllType) {
165 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
166 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
167 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
168 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
169 |       } else {
170 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
171 |       }
172 |     } else {
173 |       int size = alternates.size();
174 |       if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
175 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
176 |       } else if (size > 1) {
177 |         return null;
178 |       } else if (size == 1) {
179 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
180 |       } else {
181 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
182 |       }
183 |     }
184 |   }

===== M0340 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.jsString(String,CharsetEncoder)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 939-966
Documentation:
/** Outputs a js string, using the optimal (single/double) quote character */
Method code:
939 |   static String jsString(String s, CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder) {
940 |     int singleq = 0, doubleq = 0;
941 | 
942 |     // could count the quotes and pick the optimal quote character
943 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
944 |       switch (s.charAt(i)) {
945 |         case '"': doubleq++; break;
946 |         case '\'': singleq++; break;
947 |       }
948 |     }
949 | 
950 |     String doublequote, singlequote;
951 |     char quote;
952 |     if (singleq < doubleq) {
953 |       // more double quotes so escape the single quotes
954 |       quote = '\'';
955 |       doublequote = "\"";
956 |       singlequote = "\\\'";
957 |     } else {
958 |       // more single quotes so escape the doubles
959 |       quote = '\"';
960 |       doublequote = "\\\"";
961 |       singlequote = "\'";
962 |     }
963 | 
964 |     return strEscape(s, quote, doublequote, singlequote, "\\\\",
965 |         outputCharsetEncoder);
966 |   }
