614 |                                  boolean tagAsStrict) {
615 |     Preconditions.checkState(sourceMapDetailLevel != null);
616 | 
617 |     boolean createSourceMap = (sourceMap != null);
618 |     MappedCodePrinter mcp =
619 |         outputFormat == Format.COMPACT
620 |         ? new CompactCodePrinter(
621 |             lineBreak, lineLengthThreshold,
622 |             createSourceMap, sourceMapDetailLevel)
623 |         : new PrettyCodePrinter(
624 |             lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap, sourceMapDetailLevel);
625 |     CodeGenerator cg =
626 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED
627 |         ? new TypedCodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset)
628 |         : new CodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset);
629 | 
630 |     if (tagAsStrict) {
631 |       cg.tagAsStrict();
632 |     }
633 | 
634 |     cg.add(root);
635 |     mcp.endFile();
636 | 
637 |     String code = mcp.getCode();
638 | 
639 |     if (createSourceMap) {
640 |       mcp.generateSourceMap(sourceMap);
641 |     }
642 | 
643 |     return code;
644 |   }

===== M0344 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1597-1656
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1597 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1598 |     if (type != node.getType()
1599 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1600 |         || getNodeClass(this) != getNodeClass(node)) {
1601 |       return false;
1602 |     }
1603 | 
1604 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1605 |       return false;
1606 |     }
1607 | 
1608 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1609 |       try {
1610 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1611 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1612 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1613 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1614 |             return false;
1615 |           }
1616 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1617 |           return false;
1618 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1619 |           return false;
1620 |         } else {
1621 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1622 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1623 |               return false;
1624 |             }
1625 |           }
1626 |         }
1627 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1628 |         return false;
1629 |       }
1630 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1631 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1632 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1633 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1634 |         return false;
1635 |       }
1636 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1637 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1638 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1639 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1640 |         return false;
1641 |       }
1642 |     }
1643 | 
1644 |     if (recurse) {
1645 |       Node n, n2;
1646 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1647 |            n != null;
1648 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1649 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1650 |           return false;
1651 |         }
1652 |       }
1653 |     }
1654 | 
1655 |     return true;
1656 |   }

93 |     // Handle all binary operators

107 |       // Handle associativity.
108 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
109 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.

111 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {

113 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
114 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else {
117 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
118 |       }
119 |       return;

491 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
492 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
493 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
494 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
495 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
496 |         if (postProp != 0) {
497 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
498 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
499 |         } else {
500 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
501 |           add(first);
502 |         }
503 |         break;
504 |       }
505 | 
506 |       case Token.CALL:
507 |         // We have two special cases here:
508 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
509 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
510 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
511 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
512 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
513 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
514 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
515 |         // that must be preserved.
516 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
517 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
518 |           add("(0,");
519 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
520 |           add(")");
521 |         } else {
522 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
523 |         }
524 |         add("(");
525 |         addList(first.getNext());
526 |         add(")");
527 |         break;
528 | 
529 |       case Token.IF:
530 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
531 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
532 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
533 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
534 |           cc.beginBlock();
535 |         }
536 | 
537 |         add("if(");
538 |         add(first);
539 |         add(")");
540 | 
541 |         if (hasElse) {
542 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
543 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
544 |           add("else");
545 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
546 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
547 |         } else {
548 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
549 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
550 |         }
551 | 
552 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
553 |           cc.endBlock();
554 |         }
555 |         break;
556 | 
557 |       case Token.NULL:
558 |       case Token.THIS:
559 |       case Token.FALSE:
560 |       case Token.TRUE:
561 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
562 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
563 |         break;
564 | 
565 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
566 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
567 |         add("continue");
568 |         if (childCount == 1) {
569 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
570 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
571 |           }
572 |           add(" ");
573 |           add(first);
574 |         }
575 |         cc.endStatement();
576 |         break;
577 | 
578 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
579 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
580 |         add("debugger");
581 |         cc.endStatement();
582 |         break;
583 | 
584 |       case Token.BREAK:
585 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
586 |         add("break");
587 |         if (childCount == 1) {
588 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
589 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
590 |           }
591 |           add(" ");
592 |           add(first);
593 |         }
594 |         cc.endStatement();
595 |         break;
596 | 
597 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
598 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
599 | 
600 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
601 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
602 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
603 |         cc.endStatement();
604 |         break;
605 | 
606 |       case Token.NEW:
607 |         add("new ");
608 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
609 | 
610 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
611 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
612 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
613 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(first, Token.CALL, new MatchNotFunction())) {
614 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
615 |         }
616 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
617 | 
618 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
619 |         Node next = first.getNext();
620 |         if (next != null) {
621 |           add("(");
622 |           addList(next);
623 |           add(")");
624 |         }
625 |         break;
626 | 
627 |       case Token.STRING:
628 |         if (childCount !=
629 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
630 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0)) {
631 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
632 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
633 |         }
634 |         add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
635 |         break;
636 | 
637 |       case Token.DELPROP:
638 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
639 |         add("delete ");
640 |         add(first);
641 |         break;
642 | 
643 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
644 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
645 |         if (needsParens) {
646 |           add("(");
647 |         }
648 |         add("{");
649 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
650 |           if (c != first) {
651 |             cc.listSeparator();
652 |           }
653 | 
654 |           if (c.getType() == Token.GET || c.getType() == Token.SET) {
655 |             add(c);
656 |           } else {
657 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.getType() == Token.STRING);
658 |             String key = c.getString();
659 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
660 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
661 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
662 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
663 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
664 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
665 |                 // were unicode escaped.
666 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
667 |               add(key);
668 |             } else {
669 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
670 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
671 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
672 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
673 |               } else {
674 |                 addExpr(c, 1);
675 |               }
676 |             }
677 |             add(":");
678 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
679 |           }
680 |         }
681 |         add("}");
682 |         if (needsParens) {
683 |           add(")");
684 |         }
685 |         break;
686 |       }
687 | 
688 |       case Token.SWITCH:
689 |         add("switch(");
690 |         add(first);
691 |         add(")");
692 |         cc.beginBlock();
693 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
694 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
695 |         break;
696 | 
697 |       case Token.CASE:
698 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
699 |         add("case ");
700 |         add(first);
701 |         addCaseBody(last);
702 |         break;
703 | 
704 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
705 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
706 |         add("default");
707 |         addCaseBody(first);
708 |         break;
709 | 
710 |       case Token.LABEL:
711 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
712 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
713 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
714 |         }
715 |         add(first);
716 |         add(":");
717 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
718 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
719 |         break;
720 | 
721 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
722 |       // ignored for our purposes.
723 |       case Token.SETNAME:
724 |         break;
725 | 
726 |       default:
727 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
728 |     }
729 | 
730 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
731 |   }

===== M0356 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 734-776
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
734 |   @Override
735 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
736 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
737 |       return true;
738 |     }
739 | 
740 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
741 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
742 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
743 |         return true;
744 |       }
745 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
746 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
747 |         return false;
748 |       }
749 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
750 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
751 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
752 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
753 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
754 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
755 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
756 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
757 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
758 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
759 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
760 | 
761 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
762 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
763 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
764 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
765 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
766 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
767 | 
768 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
769 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
770 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
771 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
772 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
773 |     }
774 | 
775 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
776 |   }

===== M0384 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1073-1211
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1073 |   Node parseInputs() {
1074 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1075 | 
1076 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1077 |     // individual file parse trees.
1078 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1079 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1080 |     }
1081 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1082 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1083 |     }
1084 | 
1085 |     // Parse main js sources.
1086 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1087 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1088 | 
1089 |     externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1090 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1091 | 
1092 |     externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1093 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1094 | 
1095 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1096 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1097 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1098 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1099 |     }
1100 | 
1101 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1102 | 
1103 |     try {
1104 |       // Parse externs sources.
1105 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1106 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1107 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1108 |           return null;
1109 |         }
1110 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1111 |       }
1112 | 
1113 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1114 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1115 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1116 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1117 | 
1118 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1119 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1120 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1121 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1122 |           }
1123 |         }
1124 | 
1125 |         try {
1126 |           inputs =
1127 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1128 |               .manageDependencies(
1129 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1130 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1131 |           report(JSError.make(
1132 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1133 |           return null;
1134 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1135 |           report(JSError.make(
1136 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1137 |           return null;
1138 |         }
1139 |       }
1140 | 
1141 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1142 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1143 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1144 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1145 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1146 |           return null;
1147 |         }
1148 | 
1149 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1150 |         if (n == null) {
1151 |           continue;
1152 |         }
1153 | 
1154 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1155 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1156 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1157 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1158 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1159 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1160 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1161 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1162 | 
1163 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1164 | 
1165 |             externs.add(input);
1166 |             staleInputs = true;
1167 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1168 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1169 |             staleInputs = true;
1170 |           }
1171 |         }
1172 |       }
1173 | 
1174 |       if (staleInputs) {
1175 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1176 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1177 |       }
1178 | 
1179 |       // Build the AST.
1180 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1181 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1182 |         if (n == null) {
1183 |           continue;
1184 |         }
1185 | 
1186 |         if (devMode) {
1187 |           runSanityCheck();
1188 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1189 |             return null;
1190 |           }
1191 |         }
1192 | 
1193 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1194 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1195 | 
1196 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1197 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1198 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1199 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1200 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1201 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1202 |         }
1203 | 
1204 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1205 |       }
1206 | 
1207 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1208 |     } finally {
1209 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1210 |     }
1211 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)

/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
774 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
775 |     if (item == null) {
776 |       return null;
777 |     } else if (item.type == propType) {
778 |       return item.next;
779 |     } else {
780 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.next, propType);

782 |         return new PropListItem(

785 |         return item;
786 |       }
787 |     }
788 |   }

61 |   @Override
62 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
63 |     Node currentVersionOfNode = n;
64 |     boolean somethingChanged = false;
65 | 
66 |     do {
67 |       somethingChanged = false;
68 |       for (AbstractPeepholeOptimization optimization : peepholeOptimizations) {
69 |         Node newVersionOfNode =
70 |             optimization.optimizeSubtree(currentVersionOfNode);
71 | 
72 |         if (newVersionOfNode != currentVersionOfNode) {
73 |           somethingChanged = true;
74 | 
75 |           currentVersionOfNode = newVersionOfNode;
76 |         }
77 | 
78 |         if (currentVersionOfNode == null) {
79 |           return;
80 |         }
81 |       }
82 |     } while(somethingChanged);
83 |   }

===== M0394 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isStrWhiteSpaceChar(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 379-397
Documentation:
/**
   * Copied from Rhino's ScriptRuntime
   */
Method code:
379 |   static TernaryValue isStrWhiteSpaceChar(int c) {
380 |     switch (c) {
381 |       case '\u000B': // <VT>
382 |         return TernaryValue.UNKNOWN;  // IE says "no", EcmaScript says "yes"
383 |       case ' ': // <SP>
384 |       case '\n': // <LF>
385 |       case '\r': // <CR>
386 |       case '\t': // <TAB>
387 |       case '\u00A0': // <NBSP>
388 |       case '\u000C': // <FF>
389 |       case '\u2028': // <LS>
390 |       case '\u2029': // <PS>
391 |       case '\uFEFF': // <BOM>
392 |         return TernaryValue.TRUE;
393 |       default:
394 |         return (Character.getType(c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR)
395 |             ? TernaryValue.TRUE : TernaryValue.FALSE;
396 |     }
397 |   }

===== M0395 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.trimJsWhiteSpace(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 362-374
Documentation:

Method code:
362 |   static String trimJsWhiteSpace(String s) {
363 |     int start = 0;
364 |     int end = s.length();
365 |     while (end > 0
366 |         && isStrWhiteSpaceChar(s.charAt(end - 1)) == TernaryValue.TRUE) {
367 |       end--;
368 |     }
369 |     while (start < end
370 |         && isStrWhiteSpaceChar(s.charAt(start)) == TernaryValue.TRUE) {
371 |       start++;
372 |     }
373 |     return s.substring(start, end);
374 |   }

/**
   * Returns true if the operator is commutative.
   * e.g. (a * b) * c = c * (b * a)
   * Note 1: "+" is not commutative because it is also the concatenation
   * for strings. e.g. "a" + (1 + 2) is not "a" + 1 + 2
   * Note 2: only operations on literals and pure functions are commutative.
   */
Method code:
1366 |   static boolean isCommutative(int type) {

1370 |       case Token.BITXOR:
1371 |       case Token.BITAND:
1372 |         return true;
1373 |       default:
1374 |         return false;
1375 |     }
1376 |   }

===== M0408 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 203-231
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
204 |     maybeEndStatement();
205 | 
206 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
207 |     char prev = getLastChar();
208 | 
209 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
210 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
211 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
212 |       append(" ");
213 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
214 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
215 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
216 |       append(" ");
217 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
218 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     }
221 | 
222 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
223 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
224 | 
225 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
226 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
227 |     // because it looks strange.
228 |     if (binOp) {
229 |       maybeCutLine();
230 |     }
231 |   }

===== M0433 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.performArithmeticOp(int,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 664-744
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to fold arithmetic binary operators
   */
Method code:
664 |   private Node performArithmeticOp(int opType, Node left, Node right) {
665 |     // Unlike other operations, ADD operands are not always converted
666 |     // to Number.
667 |     if (opType == Token.ADD
668 |         && (NodeUtil.mayBeString(left, false)
669 |             || NodeUtil.mayBeString(right, false))) {
670 |       return null;
671 |     }
672 | 
673 |     double result;
674 | 
675 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Handle NaN with unknown value. BIT ops convert NaN
676 |     // to zero so this is a little akward here.
677 | 
678 |     Double lValObj = NodeUtil.getNumberValue(left);
679 |     if (lValObj == null) {
680 |       return null;
681 |     }
682 |     Double rValObj = NodeUtil.getNumberValue(right);
683 |     if (rValObj == null) {
684 |       return null;
685 |     }
686 | 
687 |     double lval = lValObj;
688 |     double rval = rValObj;
689 | 
690 |     switch (opType) {
691 |       case Token.BITAND:
692 |         result = ScriptRuntime.toInt32(lval) & ScriptRuntime.toInt32(rval);
693 |         break;
694 |       case Token.BITOR:
695 |         result = ScriptRuntime.toInt32(lval) | ScriptRuntime.toInt32(rval);
696 |         break;
697 |       case Token.BITXOR:
698 |         result = ScriptRuntime.toInt32(lval) ^ ScriptRuntime.toInt32(rval);
699 |         break;
700 |       case Token.ADD:
701 |         result = lval + rval;
702 |         break;
703 |       case Token.SUB:
704 |         result = lval - rval;
705 |         break;
706 |       case Token.MUL:
707 |         result = lval * rval;
708 |         break;
709 |       case Token.MOD:
710 |         if (rval == 0) {
711 |           return null;
712 |         }
713 |         result = lval % rval;
714 |         break;
715 |       case Token.DIV:
716 |         if (rval == 0) {
717 |           return null;
718 |         }
719 |         result = lval / rval;
720 |         break;
721 |       default:
722 |         throw new Error("Unexpected arithmetic operator");
723 |     }
724 | 
725 |     // TODO(johnlenz): consider removing the result length check.
726 |     // length of the left and right value plus 1 byte for the operator.
727 |     if (String.valueOf(result).length() <=
728 |         String.valueOf(lval).length() + String.valueOf(rval).length() + 1 &&
729 | 
730 |         // Do not try to fold arithmetic for numbers > 2^53. After that
731 |         // point, fixed-point math starts to break down and become inaccurate.
732 |         Math.abs(result) <= MAX_FOLD_NUMBER) {
733 |       Node newNumber = Node.newNumber(result);
734 |       return newNumber;
735 |     } else if (Double.isNaN(result)) {
736 |       return Node.newString(Token.NAME, "NaN");
737 |     } else if (result == Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY) {
738 |       return Node.newString(Token.NAME, "Infinity");
739 |     } else if (result == Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY) {
740 |       return new Node(Token.NEG, Node.newString(Token.NAME, "Infinity"));
741 |     }
742 | 
743 |     return null;
744 |   }

===== M0449 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 90-132
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
90 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
91 |                            String sourceString,
92 |                            Config config,
93 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
94 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
95 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
96 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
97 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
98 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
99 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
100 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
101 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
102 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
103 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
104 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
105 | 
106 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
107 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
108 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
109 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
113 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
114 |     }
115 | 
116 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
117 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
118 |     try {
119 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
120 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
121 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
122 |     } finally {
123 |       Context.exit();
124 |     }
125 |     Node root = null;
126 |     if (astRoot != null) {
127 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
128 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
129 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
130 |     }
131 |     return root;
132 |   }

Documentation:

Method code:
621 |   private void addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
622 |       String propertyName, JSType type) {
623 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType && ((ObjectType) type).hasReferenceName()) {
624 |       Map<String, ObjectType> typeSet =
625 |           eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
626 |       if (typeSet == null) {
627 |         typeSet = Maps.newHashMap();
628 |         eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
629 |       }
630 |       ObjectType objType = (ObjectType) type;
631 |       typeSet.put(objType.getReferenceName(), objType);
632 |     } else if (type instanceof NamedType) {
633 |       addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
634 |           propertyName, ((NamedType) type).getReferencedType());
635 |     } else if (type instanceof UnionType) {
636 |       for (JSType alternate : ((UnionType) type).getAlternates()) {
637 |         addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, alternate);
638 |       }
639 |     }
640 |   }

// Gets all the property types, in sorted order.
Method code:
841 |   private int[] getSortedPropTypes() {
842 |     int count = 0;
843 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.next) {
844 |       count++;
845 |     }
846 | 
847 |     int[] keys = new int[count];
848 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.next) {
849 |       count--;
850 |       keys[count] = x.type;
851 |     }
852 | 
853 |     Arrays.sort(keys);
854 |     return keys;
855 |   }

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
234 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
235 |     this.type = type;
236 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
237 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
238 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
239 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
240 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
241 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
242 |       int i = 0;
243 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
244 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
245 |         i++;
246 |       }
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
250 | 
251 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
252 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
253 |       return;
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
257 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
258 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
259 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
260 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
261 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
262 |       trace.truncateEvents();
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
266 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
267 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
268 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
269 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
270 |                     + "try/finally block. "
271 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
272 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
273 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
274 |     }
275 | 
276 |     trace.startEvent(this);
277 |   }

/**
   * Casts this to an ObjectType, or returns null if this is not an ObjectType.
   *
   * Does not change the underlying JS type. If you want to simulate JS
   * autoboxing or dereferencing, you should use autoboxesTo() or dereference().
   * Those methods may change the underlying JS type.
   */
Method code:
468 |   public ObjectType toObjectType() {
469 |     return this instanceof ObjectType ? (ObjectType) this : null;
